Improved reconstruction alone cannot select an encoder or policy: frozen semantic-feature assessments, caption realignment, native multimodal and code/knowledge preservation checks remain required. Live status: `reports/masked-visual-pretraining-v1/status.json`.

The learned three-pass character readout completed **6,378 updates**. Its final greedy result was **3/256 exact transcripts**: two real-image digit `1` answers also matched under wrong-image controls, and one rendered word (`CRANES`) was correct only with its proper image. Real-image exact match therefore did not exceed its mismatched-image prior; reliable reading remains unachieved. Component character error fell to 0.732 overall (0.941 real / 0.620 rendered), which is partial sequence fitting, not native OCR ability. The component was not promoted. Results: `reports/learned-character-sequence-v1/final-reading.json`.

`slm_lab.object_feature_probe` provides a separate semantic-feature diagnostic using original human-verified object boxes. It freezes **2,559 training / 221 validation crops** over **61 training-supported classes**, with 190 validation photo groups, fixed hash selection/caps, exact crop/parent protection and no encoder updates. The oracle boxes make this easier than localization or native VQA. A CPU float64 ridge classifier uses train-only feature statistics and a fixed regularization of 1.0 on frozen 2x2 pre-projection features. Geometry, geometry/color, blank, different-class wrong-image and matched random-encoder controls remain separate. All fitted heads are diagnostic artifacts and are never deployed.

The original own encoder scored **20.4% crop validation accuracy**, the matching random control **23.5%**, and geometry/color **8.6%**; macro accuracy was 10.9%, 12.1% and 7.8%. Random minus original was +3.17 percentage points, with a photo-group bootstrap 95% interval of **[-1.46, +7.87] points**, which includes zero. The low training accuracy (29.2% original / 41.9% random), coarse pooling and fixed ridge shrinkage prevent fully isolating encoder quality from probe limitations. This diagnostic shows no demonstrated advantage from prior encoder learning, and is not native object answering. Results: `reports/object-feature-baseline-v1/summary.json`.

Masked-image training has started after the reading process exited. `scripts/assess_masked_visual_features.py` waits on the specific live MAE process identity, verifies its completed plan, then assesses its frozen encoder on the exact same object cases. Source/runtime/config/class/input/control matching is required, and accuracy differences use 2,000 photo-group bootstrap resamples with a fixed seed. Any feature gain remains a development observation; caption realignment and native multimodal/code/knowledge checks remain necessary. The scheduler does not promote a policy. Live status: `reports/object-feature-masked-v1/status.json`.

The expanded dated Wikipedia source collection has completed at **1,999,999,533 tokens / 1,083,933 documents**: 1,801,593,708 training and 198,405,825 validation tokens. All **1,808 training / 203 validation packed shards** matched their recorded hashes (`reports/wikipedia-expanded-integrity-v1.json`). The current foundation warmup continues using its earlier fixed subset; completion of collection does not mean the full expanded corpus has been trained. The already-running scheduler admits the complete corpus only after warmup and its own frozen-source checks.

Masked-image reconstruction completed **5,724 updates / ten passes**. Fixed development reconstruction MSE was 0.602 at the endpoint. The matched object-crop probe scored **24.4%** (macro 18.1%) versus the original encoder's **20.4%** (macro 10.9%) and the matching random control's 23.5%. The paired gain was **+4.07 percentage points**, with photo-group bootstrap 95% bounds **[-0.92, +8.92] points**. These bounds include zero, and neither a reconstruction gain nor an oracle-box probe proves native image understanding. The encoder has not been promoted. Reports: `reports/object-feature-masked-v1/comparison.json`.

`slm_lab.human_dialogue_corpus` prepares the author's original [OpenAssistant final conversation export](https://huggingface.co/datasets/OpenAssistant/oasst2/blob/179dd21fc55192153d94adb0e0ce8f69e222bf75/README.md), pinned to revision `179dd21fc55192153d94adb0e0ce8f69e222bf75` and the publisher's LFS hashes. The data are Apache-2.0; no released model weights are downloaded. An audit of the ready export found 135,174 messages explicitly marked non-synthetic with no model names. Preparation still rejects unknown/synthetic provenance, deleted/unreviewed nodes, positive spam/PII/language-mismatch/task-failure labels and missing/low assistant quality ratings. It keeps top-ranked or sole reviewed human replies and preserves original multi-turn text. A bad ancestor prunes the branch.

The supplement contains **11,985 training demonstrations / 4,675,705 target tokens** across **25 languages**, and **663 validation demonstrations / 255,089 targets**. All examples fit the own 32K tokenizer and context/response limits without truncation. Original official validation tree IDs are protected, including canonical repeated root prompts and prior validation prompts; exact reserved knowledge/math/public-code questions are excluded. Tree/prompt overlap is zero. Volunteer ratings are authorship/quality evidence, not independently verified facts, executable-code correctness or clinical expertise. Source and filtering metadata: `data/human-dialogue-v1/manifest.json`.

`scripts/train_dialogue_retention.py` is queued behind the verified live extended-curriculum process and original additional-photo collector identities. After the broader curriculum finishes, the handoff completes any missing preceding capability reports, including instruction coding, before validating all nine families. It then waits for the finalized additional-photo manifest and verifies its record/image hashes. The next joint SFT pass learns the new human-captioned photos and reviewed dialogues, preserves **every prior joint code/math/instruction/image record**, and adds previously absent Commons captions. **Both language and vision train**, using the existing four GPUs, so new image data update the encoder as well as the decoder. Full held-out dialogue response loss is measured before/after. The centralized assessment registry schedules all nine native capability families plus 1,000 held-out human-pair likelihood comparisons. Existing capability/preference guards retain the preceding base when the candidate fails. A content-identical selected-model alias and selected evidence feed the unchanged reward/DPO/PPO scheduler. This local development selection does not establish independent high-end-model parity. Live status: `reports/dialogue-retention-v1/status.json`.

Preference evaluation now records **evaluation-pair feedback origin** separately from **checkpoint training feedback origin**. A policy trained with SFT can be assessed on genuine human pairs even when its SFT checkpoint has no preference-training flag. The source flags come from the evaluated records; they are not inferred from the checkpoint. A CPU integration check verifies that distinction and an identical policy/reference gives zero adjusted margin.

`slm_lab.vision_alignment_experiment` now runs a paired caption-alignment experiment on the exact own foundation **2,000-update** language snapshot. The two durable initial branches have identical language/critic tensors; one uses the original vision encoder and the other the completed masked-reconstruction encoder. Only compatible own vision tensors are transferred, excluding the temporary reconstruction decoder. Neither branch uses external model weights, and both remain **706,012,672 parameters**.

Both branches receive the same **13,306 full-image captions** (11,971 ordinary photos / 1,335 Commons images), with **940 held-out captions**, zero parent/exact-pixel split overlap and no Fashion-MNIST. The frozen source images and record hashes are checked. Three contrastive passes at 224px are followed by one vision-only caption-SFT pass at 448px; the finite budgets include 18 and 6 repeated padding examples, respectively. Every completed stage must preserve the exact non-vision tensor digest. Matched before/after native caption retrieval/likelihood, OCR and object-question assessments check the same cases, pixels and controls. This separate experiment shares GPU 2 with ongoing foundation training. Source captions can include nonvisual metadata, and these three development assessments are not a final broad benchmark. No automatic policy promotion occurs. Live status and declared plan: `reports/paired-vision-alignment-v1/status.json` and `plan.json`. Six targeted transfer/data/protocol/training checks passed; native results are still pending.

Completed optimizer states from the broad text pilot, original multimodal joint pilot and lettering joint pilot were removed after checking exact equality with their retained final model tensors and matching completed report metrics. This recovered approximately **23.85 GiB**, leaving the learned models, tokenizers and evaluations intact. Those removed optimizer histories cannot resume exactly. The audit is `reports/checkpoint-storage-cleanup-v4.json`; its recovery note records that the initial audit write failed after removal, so the removed-state hashes were not persisted. Main and paired-experiment checkpoints were untouched.

An actual full-size caption-alignment snapshot at **200 updates / 6,400 image exposures** passed the frozen-language check: all **112 vision tensors changed and stayed finite**, while the complete language/critic tensor digest remained identical to the initial own foundation snapshot. This verifies optimizer isolation, not improved image recognition or reading. The 2,000-update foundation baseline scored **1/128 caption retrieval**, **0/170 OCR exact transcriptions**, and **0/164 object answers** before this intervention. The paired stages and final native comparisons are still running. Details: `reports/paired-vision-alignment-v1/step200-language-preservation.json`.

`reports/code-coverage-audit-v1.json` checks the retained source manifests and separates data coverage from executable capability evidence. Public repository collections and the six-language CodeSearchNet corpus supply broader code exposure, but the own 1,000-update policy still passes zero local functional/efficiency tasks in both Python/JavaScript prompt formats. The C++ backend passes its twelve reference contracts; final trained-model C++ evaluation is queued. Go, Java, Rust and other-language executable correctness, repository integration and independent final comparisons remain unverified. These gaps are explicit in the completion audit.

`slm_lab.verified_code_diagnostics` now assesses **32 short original-name programs actually used by the completed standalone-code SFT experiment**. It hash-selects distinct source algorithms with source responses below 256 own-tokenizer tokens, rechecks the published gold examples in the constrained worker and uses the exact original training prompts. Matched before/after runs compare teacher-forced NLL/token accuracy with unmodified 512-token greedy generation and published-example correctness. The diagnostic cannot establish generalization or efficiency: these are training programs, and some examples are visible in the prompt. It targets the gap between lower demonstration loss and zero held-out functional coding. The selection check passed; full-size results are pending. Plan/live status: `reports/verified-code-learning-diagnostic-v1/plan.json` and `status.json`.

The completed seen-code diagnostic scored **75.9% → 98.5% teacher-forced token accuracy** and **1/32 → 30/32 syntactically valid free generations**, but only **0/32 → 12/32** passed their published source examples. Both matched reports preserve the own weights during inference. Most remaining failures have valid syntax but wrong loops, returned types/values, formulas or helper names. These are actual training examples with examples visible in prompts, so the result does not establish held-out algorithmic generalization. See `reports/verified-code-learning-diagnostic-v1/comparison.json`.

`slm_lab.code_execution_preferences` rechecked **20 incorrect own responses** against their original public source implementations and examples. Each preferred public implementation passes; each unmodified rejected response fails. Records retain source licenses, cases and exact model/diagnostic hashes, and explicitly mark **synthetic execution-derived feedback**, not human RLHF or an external teacher. Two targeted selection/preference checks passed. The isolated `scripts/train_code_execution_dpo.py` pilot uses four passes / **80 DPO updates**, a 1e-6 learning rate, frozen vision, and fresh matched seen-program plus held-out Python completion/instruction correctness and empirical efficiency checks. It shares existing GPU 1, writes reviewable results and never promotes a production policy automatically. Plan/status: `reports/code-execution-dpo-v1/plan.json` and `status.json`. Small public example checks and this short pilot cannot certify general correctness, efficiency or broad retention.

The execution-derived preference pilot completed. Seen published-example passes increased **12/32 → 14/32**, comprising **seven gains and five losses of previously correct programs**. Held-out Python correctness/efficiency remained **0/12** in both formats. Completion syntax regressed **11/12 → 9/12**, while instruction syntax improved **9/12 → 11/12**. Peak allocated CUDA memory was 15.27 GiB and the eighty training updates took 50.7 seconds. The pilot was **rejected for promotion**; its small observed-failure preference set did not establish algorithmic generalization or retention. Learned weights, labels and comparisons remain available. Its completed redundant optimizer/reference state was audited before removal, reclaiming about 10.5 GiB; exact old optimizer history cannot resume. See `reports/code-execution-dpo-v1/decision.json` and `completed-state-cleanup.json`.

The original-encoder branch completed its paired contrastive/caption stages with exact frozen language/critic preservation. Matched caption retrieval improved **1/128 → 4/128** (text-to-image 1/128 → 3/128) and caption NLL **3.240 → 2.772**. Native OCR remained **0/170 exact**, and object answers remained **0/164**; OCR character error stayed very high at 11.03. Object wrong-image likelihood gain was only 0.0084. These small development results do not establish reliable image dependence, reading or generic understanding. The masked-encoder branch is now undergoing the same baseline/training/evaluation protocol. Details: `reports/paired-vision-alignment-v1/original-comparison.json`.

`slm_lab.expanded_photos` is collecting up to **100,000 additional ordinary training photos** with original human captions from the [author-published Localized Narratives release](https://google.github.io/localized-narratives/) and original Open Images/CVDF assets. It reuses the frozen publisher annotation/metadata bytes, requires per-image CC-BY-2.0 source metadata, retains CC-BY-4.0 caption attribution, and excludes every known prior image group plus source-MD5 aliases. Official validation/test IDs remain protected; existing held-out decoded pixels and a 64-bit difference-hash screen add duplicate checks. This simple screen is not a near-duplicate certification.

New photos are retained at 768px longest side as JPEG quality90/subsampling2 to bound disk use. Both 224px/448px input hashes are computed from the decoded stored JPEG, and the raw-download SHA, object-ETag verification status, original attribution/MD5 and normalization are recorded. Full source JPEGs are not kept. The collector uses two downloads, an **8 GiB image-storage cap**, and waits below **35 GiB free** so main training has priority. It may finish below the requested image count due to storage/source/quality limits; accepted counts, not the target, determine future training data. Existing validation sets are reused, without reallocating held-out images.

Two selection/storage/perceptual checks passed, and the first **ten actual fetched JPEGs** passed byte-hash, decoded-pixel and source-field checks (`reports/expanded-photo-first-input-check-v1.json`). Collection is active; the new photos are **not yet trained or integrated into the live immutable curriculum**. Status: `data/expanded-everyday-photos-v1/progress.json`. The completed original vision branch's redundant optimizer states were audited and removed, reclaiming **6.41 GiB**, while all learned weights/evaluations remain; exact old optimizer histories cannot resume. Audit: `reports/completed-original-vision-state-cleanup-v1.json`.

`slm_lab.capability_protocol` defines the complete nine-artifact command registry for both admission and candidate assessment. The coding CLI's default already emits completion and instruction reports; the earlier statement that an instruction report was necessarily missing was incorrect. The registry now explicitly requests each code mode once, avoiding duplicate instruction evaluation while retaining every assessment. The waiting handoff fills only genuinely absent baseline reports. Only the verified waiting scheduler was stopped/reloaded; active training and collection processes were preserved. The main final optimizer history can be retired only after the main process has exited, all nine baseline reports match retained final weights, and every state/model tensor agrees. Its audit is written before removal, freeing space before a potentially disk-paused photo collector is awaited. Learned model weights remain; exact retired optimizer histories cannot resume.

After the finalized-photo joint-learning handoff and complete assessment registry were connected, the complete implementation suite passed **161 tests in 81.6 seconds** (`reports/implementation-tests-v8.log`). This verifies the declared implementation checks, not high-end performance or completion of the queued nominal-model training. The then-active main, paired vision and photo-collection processes were preserved; the joint-learning scheduler awaited its recorded prerequisites.

The paired native vision experiment completed. With exactly identical frozen language/critic tensors and matched public captions/training budgets, the masked encoder scored **0/128** image-to-text retrieval versus **4/128** original; both scored **3/128** text-to-image, **0/170** native OCR and **0/164** object answers. Masked caption NLL was slightly lower (2.736 versus 2.772), but OCR character error remained 9.02 versus 11.03, with no exact readings. The masked encoder was **not transferred/promoted** on this evidence. Results and decision: `reports/paired-vision-alignment-v1/comparison.json` and `decision.json`. Completed masked-branch optimizer histories were audited and removed to preserve disk space; learned models, tokenizers and evaluation evidence remain.

`slm_lab.caption_feature_diagnostics` inspected 512 hash-selected training captions on the same frozen own language snapshot. Final-EOS representations used by the current contrastive target had mean pairwise cosine **0.989** and normalized centered variance **0.0105**. Last-content features had cosine 0.768/variance 0.231; content means had cosine **0.694**/variance **0.306**. No tensors changed. One geometry check passed. These results support a future pooling-objective ablation, without proving semantic accuracy or an improved native model. The active/shared trainer was not changed. Report: `reports/caption-feature-geometry-v1.json`.

`slm_lab.content_caption_training` adds an isolated content-mean contrastive ablation. It averages own hidden states over caption content, excluding BOS/EOS/padding, while keeping language/critic weights frozen. Its output wrapper is rebound only inside the dedicated experimental process and restored afterward; shared trainer files and running workers are unchanged. Two targeted checks passed, including actual optimizer updates with exact frozen-language preservation. No parameters or external teachers are added.

`scripts/train_content_caption_ablation.py` starts from the **same own 2,000-update original-vision checkpoint** and reuses the exact 13,306 captions, seed, three-pass 224px contrastive budget and one-pass 448px caption-SFT budget from the completed EOS-target control. `slm_lab.content_caption_evaluation` measures **both** control and candidate with the same new content-mean readout, while retaining the default EOS readout and native caption likelihood/OCR/object assessments as separate evidence. More diverse features or a readout-matching retrieval gain cannot alone justify promotion. Training and evaluation have completed without production promotion. It used existing GPU 2 and preserved every non-vision tensor after each training stage. Plan/status: `reports/content-caption-ablation-v1/plan.json` and `status.json`.

At **200 content-objective updates / 6,400 image exposures**, the actual nominal-model snapshot passed exact language/critic preservation, with all **112 vision tensors changed and finite**. This verifies isolated optimization only; neither the lower contrastive training loss nor the readout change establishes native image capability. The matched content-feature control measured **1/128 image-to-text and 2/128 text-to-image** under the new readout, separately from its earlier default EOS scores. Audit: `reports/content-caption-ablation-v1/step200-language-preservation.json`.

The actual foundation warmup **4,000-update** snapshot passed finite-weight/optimizer checks: all learned tensors and **654 optimizer tensors** were finite, data/tokenizer/stage identities matched the frozen plan, and tied embedding values agreed. This stage has processed **1,048,320,000 new target-token exposures**, not that many distinct new documents or verified skills. The 4,785-update warmup and subsequent full-corpus/instruction/multimodal/feedback phases remain active or pending. Integrity evidence: `reports/foundation-step4000-integrity-v1.json`.

The content-pooling ablation completed **1,248 contrastive updates**, **1,664 caption-SFT updates** and its matched native evaluations. Content-mean retrieval increased **1/128 → 2/128** image-to-text and **2/128 → 5/128** text-to-image, but default EOS retrieval decreased **4/128 → 1/128** and **3/128 → 1/128**. Native OCR remained **0/170 exact** and object answers **0/164**; OCR character error worsened **11.03 → 16.70**. Small likelihood gains and readout changes do not justify promotion. Exact language/critic tensors were preserved. Results: `reports/content-caption-ablation-v1/comparison.json`.

The initial image-response curriculum contains **38,032 examples**: **11,971 ordinary-photo captions**, **18,831 real word crops**, **5,000 photographed text regions**, **1,382 document page/region examples**, **348 scientific-image captions**, and **500 Fashion-MNIST categories**. Fashion-MNIST is only 1.31% of these examples. The separate 18,316-image visual pretraining corpus and additional-photo collector contain no Fashion-MNIST. The main immutable curriculum retains its existing inputs while additions are prepared separately.

`slm_lab.expanded_scene_words` has prepared **23,847 additional real word crops** from **2,143 already admitted training photos**, stopping at the **2 GiB** storage cap. These are unused published human word annotations, not new independent scenes or generated labels. Existing word IDs and held-out parent/input-pixel hashes are protected. Original JPEGs and pinned annotation bytes are verified; the original TextOCR annotation endpoint was blocked, so equivalence of the annotation mirror to that endpoint remains unverified. Dataset: `data/expanded-scene-words-v1/manifest.json`; status is prepared, **not trained**.

The verified waiting retention scheduler now admits finalized additional photos and word crops after the main curriculum and its nine native assessment artifacts complete. It first trains vision only on the **18,831 original plus 23,847 new words**, at 448px, with exact frozen language/critic preservation. It then trains all model weights on the full previous code/math/instruction/image rehearsal, reviewed public human dialogues, missing Commons captions, finalized photos and additional words. All nine capability assessments plus offline human-preference guards decide whether to retain the base or select the candidate before fresh RLHF. Queue status: `reports/dialogue-retention-v1/status.json`; final training counts and budgets are written after prerequisite datasets finish. Queued training does not establish general image understanding or reading.

The full implementation suite passed **165 tests in 82.56 seconds** after these additions (`reports/implementation-tests-v9.log`). All **23,847** additional word records passed stored-byte hashes, original annotation label/box and parent-split checks; **32 hash-selected crops** were independently reconstructed from cached original photos and matched stored RGB and 224px/448px input hashes. Audit: `reports/expanded-scene-word-input-check-v1.json`. This verifies preparation and implementation, not recognition quality.

`scripts/assess_foundation_warmup.py` measures the completed 4,785-update foundation stage against its exact starting policy. It evaluates the same **64 held-out 4,096-token windows per source**, **24 Python/JavaScript tasks in each of completion and instruction formats**, and **559 reserved domain questions** on both models. It requires identical evaluator/runtime, tokenizer, cases, budgets and precision; verifies the completed training plan and exact preservation of vision tensors; and records lost previously correct programs. Actual-report matching and three deliberately mismatched case/budget/precision checks passed. The assessment uses existing GPU 2 alongside the ongoing main job and does not select or promote a policy. Its plan, live status and eventual matched comparison are under `reports/foundation-warmup-assessment-v1/`. Repeated development tasks and exact-text source filters do not establish independent generalization or high-end-model parity.

The foundation warmup has now **completed all 4,785 updates**, with **1,254,052,800 target-token exposures**, and its retained checkpoint matches the recorded completed plan (`reports/extended-curriculum-v1/foundation-warmup-completed.json`). Its redundant completed optimizer state was retired only after the warmup workers exited and all retained/state learned tensors matched, reclaiming **7.69 GiB**. The audit was written before removal; exact old optimizer history cannot resume, while learned weights and reports remain. Audit: `reports/foundation-warmup-completed-state-cleanup-v1.json`. The next frozen larger corpus contains **2,325,601,312 distinct training-token positions** and schedules **17,746 updates / two passes / 4,650,871,680 target exposures**, followed by code instructions, verified programs, math, visual realignment, reading and joint learning. These are training budgets, not established capabilities.

`slm_lab.checkpoint_archive` stores completed unused experimental models as **lossless byte archives**. Exact common tensor bytes refer to a retained original control checkpoint; different tensors and all file metadata are stored separately. Actual byte comparisons authorize sharing, and reconstructed whole-file SHA-256 must equal the original before removing the redundant full file. Archives preserve exact research weights and old file hashes, but the original `model.pt` must be restored before loading that experiment. **Seven completed historical vision models** use this format: paired masked contrastive/caption, content-pooling contrastive/caption, original multimodal contrastive/image-reading, and lettering warmup. Together they reclaim **18.38 GiB**. Active main/feedback checkpoints are unchanged. Keep the four recorded source anchors intact: paired original contrastive/caption, original multimodal instruction, and original human-feedback DPO. Archive inventories record each precise anchor path/hash, payload hash and reconstruction segments in `model.pt.archive/manifest.json`.

The first actual full-model roundtrip restored the original file hash and **all 332 model tensors exactly** (`reports/checkpoint-archive-full-model-roundtrip-v1.json`). Targeted tests cover differing metadata offsets and vision tensors, restoration, rejection of changed anchors/payloads, existing output protection and refusal to archive incomplete jobs. Combined inventory: `reports/completed-vision-checkpoint-archives-v1.json`. To restore one archived model:

```bash
.venv/bin/python -m slm_lab.checkpoint_archive restore \
  --archive checkpoints/paired-vision-alignment-v1/masked/caption/model.pt.archive \
  --output checkpoints/paired-vision-alignment-v1/masked/caption/model.pt
```

Restoration requires enough disk for the original full checkpoint and refuses to overwrite an existing file. This storage change helps the remaining curriculum fit; it does not improve or quantize the model.

The retention and fresh-RLHF waiting schedulers were verified idle with no children/plan, stopped and reloaded to retire completed redundant optimizer states before subsequent stages. Joint-retention retirement occurs after all capability/preference assessments and selection; reward retirement follows held-out ranking, and DPO/PPO retirement follows policy-preference assessment. Every retirement requires exact retained/state learned tensors and writes an audit before removal. All learned weights, source/reference policies and assessment reports remain. This prevents accumulated optimizer/reference copies from consuming the remaining curriculum's disk reserve; exact retired optimizer history cannot resume. Reload audit: `reports/storage-safe-waiting-scheduler-reload-v1.json`. The complete archive-enabled implementation suite passed **169 tests in 95.52 seconds** (`reports/implementation-tests-v10.log`), followed by **19 targeted checks** after the waiting-scheduler cleanup changes (`reports/storage-safe-handoff-tests-v1.log`).

The completed matched foundation assessment confirms **lower held-out token loss without useful coding**. Code NLL improved **1.608 → 0.866** and Wikipedia **4.723 → 3.052**, but functional/efficiency pass rates stayed **0/24** in each code format. Completion syntax fell **9/24 → 3/24**; instruction syntax stayed **0/24**. On all **559** reserved questions, generated accuracy fell **3.94% → 2.68%**, while forced-choice accuracy rose **12.34% → 13.42%** (95% interval **10.84–16.49%**, including **11.18%** mean chance). Vision tensors were preserved exactly; this language-only stage does not establish better vision. Comparison: `reports/foundation-warmup-assessment-v1/comparison.json`. The longer 17,746-update pretraining stage is live; code instructions, verified programs, math, visual realignment, reading, broader-photo/dialogue joint learning and fresh guarded RLHF remain pending.

The actual 706M-parameter warmup model was checked with **four failing code prompts**, **32-token trajectories**, full recomputation versus KV-cache decoding, and **both FP32 and BF16**. All **128** compared next-token decisions agreed in each precision, and all four independently generated sequences matched their recomputed counterparts. Maximum absolute logit difference was **1.53e-5 FP32** and **0.125 BF16**. Weak/repetitive code persisted under full FP32 recomputation, so these cases do not identify a cache defect explaining the failure. This bounded text check does not certify long contexts or images. No weights or shared inference code changed. Report: `reports/nominal-cache-precision-diagnostic-v1.json`.

The separate **mature verified-code probe** tests whether more foundation pretraining makes the same small supervised code budget effective. It starts from the exact completed **4,785-update** weights and uses the original **2,692 variant rows / 673 public source programs**, **two passes / 673 SFT updates / 832,152 target tokens**, the same batch/accumulation and **1e-5** learning rate as the earlier 1,000-update probe. It shares the immutable source file by hardlink, trains language only on existing GPU 2, verifies exact frozen vision tensors, runs matched 24-task completion/instruction correctness and empirical efficiency checks, and retires its completed redundant optimizer state after assessment. It does not promote a policy automatically. Plan/status: `reports/mature-verified-code-probe-v1/`.

`scripts/assess_mature_code_learning.py` waited on the verified live mature-probe process, refreshed the older SFT model's held-out code reports under the current exact evaluator, and compared 32 identical original-name seen-program cases before/after the new SFT against the existing sealed older seen-program control. It checks equal source programs, budgets, tokenizer, precision, evaluator and case identities. Actual-report identity and three mismatched budget/case/precision rejection checks passed (`reports/mature-code-comparison-guard-check-v1.json`). This separates memorization, teacher forcing and held-out generation; seen published examples do not establish unseen correctness. Completed status/plan: `reports/mature-code-learning-v1/`.

The actual mature-code **200-update** snapshot passed finite-model and exact data/tokenizer/architecture checks. All **112 vision tensors** and the unused critic were unchanged, while **219 language state entries** changed; no source examples were truncated (`reports/mature-verified-code-probe-v1/snapshot-integrity.json`). This verifies the intended optimization, not correct programs. The explicit code-mode registry passed **16 targeted checks**, including invoking the real coding CLI with its evaluator mocked and verifying exactly one output per requested format (`reports/explicit-code-mode-registry-tests-v1.log`). Only its verified waiting retention scheduler was reloaded; active training remained live.

The mature verified-code intervention completed all **673 SFT updates in 852.72 seconds**, with peak allocated CUDA memory **13.24 GiB** and zero truncated source examples. All **112 vision tensors** remained exact. Completion syntax improved **3/24 → 16/24**, and instruction syntax **0/24 → 10/24**; functional/efficiency passes stayed **0/24 in both formats**. This did not establish useful algorithmic generation and was not promoted (`reports/mature-verified-code-probe-v1/comparison.json`). Completed redundant optimizer state was audited before retirement, reclaiming **7.69 GiB** while retaining final weights.

The pretraining-depth comparison also completed. With the identical verified-code SFT budget, **seen published-example passes increased 12/32 → 20/32**, comprising **nine gains and one loss**, and teacher-forced token accuracy **98.53% → 99.39%**. Both seen-program syntax rates were **30/32**. The newer foundation before SFT passed **0/32**. Fresh matched older/newer held-out code assessments stayed **0/24 functional/efficiency** in both formats, with only one additional syntax-valid program per mode (completion **15/24 → 16/24**, instruction **9/24 → 10/24**). More foundation training improved fitting of these demonstrations, without measured algorithmic generalization. Original source examples were visible in seen-program prompts. Evidence: `reports/mature-code-learning-v1/comparison.json` and `seen-case-transitions.json`. Neither experimental policy was promoted; the main longer curriculum remains live.

`slm_lab.exercise_code` adds original [Exercism programming exercises](https://exercism.org/docs/building/tracks/practice-exercises) with their published task instructions, starter interfaces, reference implementation files and test artifacts. Seven direct GitHub archives (shared specifications plus Python, JavaScript, Go, Java, Rust and C++ tracks) are pinned to exact commits and archive/license hashes. No external model weights or teacher are used; source authorship and all per-file rights are not independently exhaustively audited. Downloaded source is read as data, with selected Python references executed only by the existing restricted worker.

The finalized corpus contains **599 training implementations across 181 exercise groups** and **181 validation implementations across 53 groups**, with an exercise held in one split across all languages. Training counts are Python86/JavaScript123/Go124/Java109/Rust82/C++75; validation counts are Python25/JavaScript37/Go42/Java35/Rust24/C++18. All complete admitted examples fit the own tokenizer/context with **zero truncation**; training responses contain **350,625 target tokens**. One reserved Python-reference structural alias blocks its exercise group. **327 selected published literal reference cases** passed, but that is not all original tests, other-language execution or efficiency. All **4,608 source-file records** and **907 stored test artifacts** were audited against the pinned source bytes. Dataset/audit: `data/exercism-code-v1/manifest.json` and `reports/exercism-input-audit-v1.json`. These data are **prepared, not trained**.

`slm_lab.exercise_evaluation` tests complete generated Python modules with original task/starter interfaces against supported published literal assertions. It keeps gold implementations and test artifacts out of the explicit model prompt, reports nonempty Python syntax separately from constrained-worker functionality, and records cases/budgets/provenance. The existing mature-code candidate passed **0/6 supported validation modules (7 tested entry points)**, with **4/6** parseable nonempty outputs. Other validation records require unsupported classes/error interfaces or languages; earlier pretraining/semantic overlap is not exhaustively audited. This is additional local development evidence, not a complete or independent benchmark. Report: `reports/exercism-mature-code-baseline-v1.json`; its exact original evaluator source is retained at `reports/source-snapshots/exercise-evaluation-baseline-v1.py`.

The verified waiting joint-learning handoff now retains all **599** training implementations with **four explicitly recorded exposures each** (2,396 records; 1,402,500 response-target exposures before sampler remainders), alongside every prior rehearsal, new photos/words and reviewed dialogue. Original code targets, attribution and held-out groups stay intact. Before/after assessment adds full validation-response loss across all six languages and supported Python module execution. The full implementation suite passed **177 tests in 84.71 seconds** after source ingestion, explicit CLI-emission checks and extra exercise-guard coverage (`reports/implementation-tests-v11.log`). `slm_lab.object_supervision_data` prepares direct object-label supervision from original [Open Images boxes](https://storage.googleapis.com/openimages/web/download_v7.html). It freezes the first **30,000** already admitted additional training-photo records and combines their original box annotations with the prior training photos. Source orientation, stored image bytes, original box/metadata hashes, human annotation origin/confidence and clear-instance flags are checked. Conflicting near-identical class boxes and duplicate/conflicting input crops are excluded; one crop per class per parent and a class cap bound selection. It adds no Fashion-MNIST data or pretrained image weights.

The result contains **14,508 training crops across the same 61 classes** as the earlier object feature probe. All **221 original validation crops from 190 parents** are preserved exactly, including 224px/448px decoded input hashes, with zero train/validation parent overlap. The crop files occupy about **738 MiB**, and all recorded bytes/class indices/source splits were audited (`reports/object-supervision-input-audit-v1.json`). Dataset: `data/object-supervision-v1/manifest.json`. These supplied object boxes make the task **oracle-crop classification**, not object detection, native VQA or general image understanding. Label hierarchy/errors, rare classes and independent final generalization remain limitations.

`slm_lab.object_supervision` trains two matched branches from the own completed foundation's identical image weights: **classification head only with frozen vision**, then **head plus vision encoder**. Both receive the same seed, uniform-class/example sampling and flip/brightness/color augmentation stream, **2,721 updates / 87,072 sample exposures**, and a **1e-4** learning-rate schedule. The own linear label head has 31,293 training-only parameters. The unused language projector is frozen, language/critic tensors are not used or updated, and no full-model transfer/promotion occurs. Held classification includes correct, blank and mismatched images, macro class accuracy and a paired bootstrap over original photo parents. Declared sources/runtime are locked and final dataset/base bytes rechecked. Status/plan: `reports/object-supervision-v1/`.

The frozen-encoder control completed, reaching **27/221 (12.22%)** correct-image accuracy and **6.55%** macro accuracy, versus **0/221** blank and **17/221 (7.69%)** wrong-image matches to the original target. This is a weak auxiliary baseline; neither crop/head fitting nor image sensitivity proves reliable multimodal responses. The encoder-update branch remains active. Three targeted supervision checks passed, including actual optimization with frozen/control/projector preservation, and the full implementation suite passed **180 tests in 82.19 seconds** (`reports/implementation-tests-v12.log`). Main language training and ordinary-photo collection were preserved.

The paired supervised-object experiment has completed. Updating the encoder/head improved correct-image accuracy **27/221 → 63/221 (12.22% → 28.51%)**, macro accuracy **6.55% → 21.85%**, and NLL **3.614 → 2.663**. The paired gain was **16.29 percentage points**, with a 2,000-resample parent-photo bootstrap interval of **10.31–22.42 points**. The updated model's blank-image target matches were **10/221 (4.52%)**, and mismatched-image matches **0/221**. Language/critic weights were not used or updated, and the original projector remained exact. This supports improved **oracle-crop classification**, while detector/localization, native answers, text reading and broad generalization remain unverified. Evidence: `reports/object-supervision-v1/comparison.json`. No production transfer occurred.

`scripts/train_supervised_vision_alignment.py` tests whether this own encoder improves native responses. It verifies that the frozen classifier control started from exactly the same vision tensors as the earlier paired original branch, composes only the supervised vision tensors into the same **2,000-update own language snapshot**, and excludes the classification head. It reuses the original **13,306 captions**, seed, **1,248-update 224px contrastive** and **1,664-update 448px caption-SFT** budgets, preserving every language/critic tensor exactly. Original control reports are admitted only with exact checkpoint and current evaluator provenance. Native caption retrieval/likelihood, OCR and object responses must use identical cases/pixels/controls. The experiment is active on existing GPU 2; the main long curriculum remains unchanged. Plan/status: `reports/supervised-vision-alignment-v1/`. Auxiliary classification gains alone cannot justify production selection.

Checkpoint archival now also accepts a prepared **weights-only** snapshot when its exact own preparation receipt identifies the file hash, all weights are finite, and no optimizer/reference state or active user of that checkpoint is present. Use the existing restore command before reusing an archived model path.


The complete-module C++ assessment now runs all **18** held-out Exercism C++ implementations against their original **265 published test cases / 326 assertions**, with `EXERCISM_RUN_ALL_TESTS` enabled. Original archive/revision, solution interfaces, test bytes and Catch harness are checked; compiler and runtime both use the existing fail-closed Landlock/seccomp launcher. ```

The own mature coding checkpoint completed this additional assessment: **0/18 complete C++ modules**. All18 responses failed the requested full-file format/language before compilation, often returning malformed Python. These are module-delivery failures, not a measured raw C++ syntax rate; reference passes are not model scores. This checks complete library interfaces, classes/templates, exceptions and published behavior, while efficiency, broader repositories, all-language coverage and independent final capability remain unverified. Header/preprocessor restrictions can reject otherwise valid programs, and same-process JUnit checks are not proof against adversarial score forgery. After adding the matched C++ retention helper and safely reloading the waiting handoff, the full implementation suite passed **202 tests in 99.10 seconds** (`reports/implementation-tests-v14.log`). `scripts/train_exercise_module_probe.py --preceding-pid 185199` prepares a separate **six-language complete-module experiment** from the own completed mature coding weights. ### Original human questions about real photographs

`scripts/inspect_vqa_sources.py` inspected the original [VQA v2](https://visualqa.org/download.html) questions, ten human answers per question and complementary-pair lists, plus original COCO2014 image/license metadata. The VQA annotations declare CC-BY4.0; image licenses are separate. Filtering to original image license4 (CC-BY2.0) and at least6/10 exact human agreement leaves54,007 training questions and26,264 validation questions. Requiring the same question, different images and different agreed answers retains3,052 training pairs across4,242 photos and1,574 validation pairs across2,192 photos. Candidate metadata and source hashes are retained, but images/attribution/protected overlaps must still be checked before training. This filtered subset is not the full standardized benchmark. Report: `reports/vqa-original-source-inspection-v1.json`.

`slm_lab.vqa_attribution` parses current original Flickr JSON-LD as data, checks the requested photo identity, CC-BY2.0 declaration and matching creator/profile, and rejects missing or conflicting declarations. No VQA images or rows have been admitted to training.

`slm_lab.vqa_pairs` is collecting original real-photo question pairs, retaining current creator/license declarations as compressed original HTML plus original COCO image download hashes/ETag checks and stored JPEG/input-pixel hashes. Official source-photo alias screening retains2,994 training-pair candidates and1,273 validation-pair candidates; it protects all known OpenImages/COCO official opposing split aliases, including COCO2015 test images. Collection selects all surviving training pairs and at most512 validation pairs (5,043 distinct source image IDs), bounded by1.5 GiB and35 GiB free disk. Final admission waits for completed additional-photo inputs, then applies exact/alias/perceptual protection and retains only complete pairs. Both images receive the identical canonical question; original questions and all ten human answer records remain metadata. Duplicate question IDs share one training row with multiple pair references. No generated captions, external teacher or model weights are used.

The preparatory null-alias screen was rejected and preserved before collection; missing identities no longer match unrelated photos (`reports/vqa-official-image-alias-screen-invalid-null-alias-v1.json`). The corrected full original-metadata screen passed (`reports/vqa-official-image-alias-screen-v1.json`). Ten actual stored photos/pages passed byte/creator/license/224px/448px checks (`reports/vqa-first-stored-input-check-v1.json`), which does not certify the whole unfinished corpus. Flickr subsequently returned429 even for a previously successful source photo. The collector was stopped, its exact source/config/log retained, and95 committed image/page records verified before explicit resume. It now spaces Flickr page requests by at least10 seconds, observes an initial600-second cooldown and honors subsequent Retry-After values without dropping temporarily rate-limited sources.33 focused checks passed (`reports/vqa-rate-limit-and-source-tests-v1.log`). The live collection is not finalized or trained; recovery evidence: `reports/vqa-rate-limit-recovery-v1.json`; progress: `data/vqa-real-pairs-v1/progress.json`.

`slm_lab.clevr_scene_evaluation` directly generates full descriptions on12 hash-selected, unique held-out scenes, using identical task prompts and proper/blank/rotated-image controls. It scores complete scene correctness, object count, declared shape counts, projected-order attribute matches, attribute multiset F1 and internal count consistency. All blank generations must agree, and every rotated-image generation must equal that counterpart's proper generation under the identical task. Original targets are never supplied to generation. Six strict parser/metric checks and the full301-test suite passed (`reports/implementation-tests-v27.log`).
