After the completed1,000-update vision-only stage (622,772 targets, exact language/critic preservation), proper-image object counts were correct6/12 versus2/12 blank and1/12 wrong-image original-target matches. Ordered attribute credit was41.28% proper versus22.49% blank/23.34% wrong, while attribute-multiset F1 remained only8.45% and full-scene/declared-shape-count exactness stayed0. This is a preliminary signal on a small reused development sample, not reliable detection or general reasoning. Case/prompt/pixel/control matching was verified in `reports/clevr-dense-learning-v1/format-vs-vision-scene-comparison.json`. The original-question stage has now completed: native QA scored **101/247 proper**, **107/247 blank** and **104/247 wrong-image original-target matches**, with no reliable image advantage. The subsequent description check scored **0/12 valid descriptions**, showing format forgetting after QA-only SFT. The candidate failed the prior QA retention guard and was not promoted (`reports/clevr-dense-learning-v1/comparison.json`, `questions-scene-generation.json`).

Published OpenImages creator/license metadata were also cross-matched to VQA photo identities without querying rate-limited Flickr. It yielded672 provisional source credit records, but only36 training pairs and2 validation pairs after opposing OpenImages split protection. That coverage is insufficient to replace the planned broader paired evaluation. Credits remain unadmitted pending consistent source identity, full image-byte and official-split checks. Report: `reports/vqa-published-credit-crosscheck-v1.json`; the original paced collector continues to respect publisher cooldowns.


`slm_lab.clevr_joint_rehearsal` prepares the **exact same seed-42 first 10,000 QA exposures** used in the prior experiment together with all **4,000 original scene descriptions**. It rechecks original input receipts, stored photo hashes, own-tokenizer targets and validation groups. The 14,000-record pass contains **336,093 target tokens**, with no truncation or held-out image-group overlap. `scripts/train_clevr_joint_rehearsal.py` is running **1,750 joint updates** from the retained vision-stage checkpoint, using batch2/accumulation4, LR2e-5,448px and existing GPU2 alongside the main run. It measures native QA before/after and compares actual free descriptions against both vision-only and QA-only baselines. The extra500 updates/311,386 description targets and roughly92.65% description share of token-weighted supervision are explicit; this is not a matched-budget superiority test. No automatic promotion occurs.

`slm_lab.textvqa_data` prepares original human reading questions from the publisher's actual [TextVQA release](https://textvqa.org/dataset/), downloaded directly from `dl.fbaipublicfiles.com`; both complete JSON hashes match the recorded release. The pinned publisher declaration licenses annotations under CC-BY-4.0; reused Open Images photos retain their distinct author credits and CC-BY-2.0 licenses. It admits **5,858 training questions on4,263 existing full photos** and **468 validation questions on345 photos**, requiring at least six identical original human answers. Full-frame photo IDs/Flickr aliases, all prepared opposing image groups/pixels, actual stored file hashes and complete own-tokenizer sequences are checked. No image downloads or external OCR/model weights were added. This corpus is **scheduled for joint retention training, not yet trained**. Its validation uses existing development photos, rather than an independent final benchmark (`data/textvqa-human-reading-v1/manifest.json`).

`slm_lab.textvqa_evaluation` assesses **128 hash-selected distinct real photos** with original questions,32-token native greedy generation and proper/blank/different-image controls. Original human answers and image-class labels remain metadata only; the actual encoder input contains the photo and question. Exact answer scores and transparently limited ten-answer agreement are reported separately from likelihood. `scripts/assess_textvqa_joint_transfer.py` is verified waiting on the exact live rehearsal controller, then evaluates its own vision base and completed candidate for zero-shot transfer to real-photo reading. It does not train on TextVQA or promote the candidate. **29 focused checks passed**, including source-photo identity, human consensus, absence of OCR/answer/class hints, unique-photo assessment and matched scene-retention evidence (`reports/native-reading-and-joint-retention-tests-v1.log`). The previous full suite remains301 tests; these focused results are not a final capability claim.

`slm_lab.textvqa_input_audit` completed a full recheck of **6,326 original human questions and4,608 actual full photographs**. Every retained question/ten-answer list/consensus target is matched to the publisher JSON; each input is bound to its actual previous full-photo record, original author/license and stored bytes. Both224px/448px image hashes and complete own-tokenizer prompt/label counts match. The audit shares the project's encoder/pixel/alias helpers; it is not an independent reimplementation (`reports/textvqa-original-input-audit-v1.json`). `slm_lab.textvqa_admission` requires that completed audit and rechecks current source inventories and every actual photo byte hash before queued training. Sixteen focused checks passed, including changed photo bytes, stale audited manifests, crop substitution, invented author credits and leaked-answer prompts.

The idle `train_dialogue_retention.py` scheduler was safely reloaded after verifying its exact process identity, waiting status and absence of children, preserving its prior source. It now adds all5,858 human reading questions to full joint rehearsal, with no duplicate prior question IDs and all4,263 photo parents already admitted to training. Before/after native reading reports and a per-question retention guard participate in policy selection, and the selected policy's reading report is retained. Startup also accepts the finalized photo manifest after the downloader has completed. The live main trainer and other experiment/feedback schedulers continued unchanged. Reload evidence: `reports/dialogue-retention-v1/textvqa-admission-reload-v1.json`. The complete implementation suite passed **323 tests in82.41s** (`reports/implementation-tests-v28.log`); reliable broad model capability remains unverified.

The interleaved scene/QA intervention completed **1,750 updates /14,000 record exposures /336,093 actual target tokens**, without truncation. It preserved description format on12/12 scenes, but full scenes and declared shape counts remained incorrect, and object-count correctness fell from6/12 to2/12. Native questions scored **110/247 proper,110/247 blank,107/247 wrong-image original-target matches**. It lost72 previously correct QA-only cases despite higher total accuracy and failed count retention; the candidate was not promoted. Matched results: `reports/clevr-joint-rehearsal-v1/comparison.json`.

Real-photo transfer evaluation completed on the same128 original validation photos before/after. Exact answers rose from0/128 to **5/128 proper**, versus1/128 blank and3/128 wrong. **All five correct answers were the literal `yes`**: numeric answers stayed0/24 and other original strings0/95. The paired control differences were not significant at5% (exact two-sided discordance probabilities0.21875 blank /0.625 wrong), and the overall result remains below the6.25% answer-mode baseline. This does not establish reading/transcribing image text. Scope and original questions/control outputs are retained in `reports/textvqa-joint-transfer-v1/correct-answer-analysis.json`; alternative images are not newly annotated for the current question.

`slm_lab.feedback_reading_guard` extends the queued feedback branch selection with all128 distinct real-photo cases. DPO and reward-gated PPO must preserve every previously correct native reading answer alongside all existing nine-family capability and offline human-pair criteria; a failed existing guard cannot be overridden by reading success. The selected SFT reading report is reused only for the exact policy/data/manifest/current evaluator, otherwise a fresh baseline is generated. The idle feedback scheduler was identity/status/no-child verified and safely reloaded with `--real-photo-reading-guard`; main and SFT handoff training continued (`reports/extended-human-feedback-v1/real-photo-reading-guard-reload-v1.json`). Six new guard checks and20 focused combined selection checks passed; the full suite passed **329 tests in83.70s** (`reports/implementation-tests-v29.log`). Feedback training remains queued, not newly completed.

Six inactive completed research models were preserved as exact XOR archives, recovering **4,408,357,735 payload bytes** before manifest overhead. Every original checkpoint byte was reconstructed and its whole-file SHA verified before removing the redundant full file; referenced full anchors and shared hard links were preserved. Summary and anchor dependencies: `reports/inactive-model-exact-xor-archives-v2.json`. Restore archived policies with `python -m slm_lab.checkpoint_xor restore --archive <model.pt.xor-directory> --output <fresh-model.pt-path>` before using them. This storage recovery is not training/capability evidence.

The unpromoted completed lettering model was subsequently archived byte-exactly against the retained original DPO model, saving another489,520,648 bytes. Combined recovery is **4,897,878,383 payload bytes** before manifest overhead; full required anchors remain (`reports/lettering-completed-exact-xor-v2.json`). The35GiB collection reserve was restored, but publisher Flickr cooldowns still apply; the real-photo pair corpus is not finalized. A four-crop visual spotcheck found two clearly readable original words and two blurry handwritten crops, with both original source-image dimensions matching the annotation metadata. This limited inspection did not reveal a crop-coordinate mismatch and does not certify corpus-wide readability (`reports/real-word-crop-visual-spotcheck-v1.json`).

`slm_lab.exposure_audit` completed the read-only full-question/specification exposure check for all **559 retained knowledge cases,1,319 official math questions and175 public-code specifications**. It scanned the actual packed older/warmup/current/queued code corpora and **1,119,010 learner-visible SFT sequences** with the original role formatting, coordinate transforms and truncation. **Zero exact retained question/specification matches were found.** Incremental UTF-8 decoding and normalized-character carry preserve Unicode and arbitrarily long whitespace across chunks/shards; EOS/BOS/PAD boundaries prevent joining unrelated documents. Actual shard bytes and source/evaluator implementation are bound by hashes. Six boundary/Unicode/corrupted-source checks passed, and byte decoding matched the original tokenizer on100,000 actual training tokens. The fixed evaluation files are unchanged. Paraphrases, shared solutions and semantic overlap are outside this check; it does not certify independent generalization. Current source plans include not-yet-trained tokens (`reports/expanded-training-exposure-audit-v1/audit.json`).

`slm_lab.byte_word_reader` adds a **2,115,105-parameter scratch CNN/two-layer bidirectional LSTM/CTC recognizer**, motivated by the [original CRNN work](https://arxiv.org/abs/1507.05717). It uses blank plus256 UTF-8 byte classes, fixed32x512 RGB input independent of target length,128 output frames and a64-byte transcript budget. No pretrained weights or tokenizer are imported. Hypothetical attachment to the own policy would total **708,127,777 parameters**, but the recognizer is currently a standalone word-crop component and does not locate text in whole photos.

Preparation references **91,585 existing training crops** and3,429 validation crops without copying image data; protected rendered-validation words exclude292 additional training records. All256 earlier character-validation case identities remain available. `scripts/train_byte_word_reader.py` is running eight passes /**11,456 updates** on existing GPU3, with a finite732,680-example budget, proper/blank/wrong-image reading checks before/after, BF16 convolutions and FP32 recurrent/CTC computation. An actual eight-crop CUDA update produced finite gradients at258,286,592 peak allocated bytes; every initial tensor was then restored exactly before real training. The new input resolution, architecture, byte alphabet and additional budget are not matched to the older character experiment. No native model promotion or whole-photo reading success is claimed. Three byte/alignment/gradient checks and the full **338-test suite** passed (`reports/implementation-tests-v30.log`); learning and full exposure-audit results remain pending.

`scripts/assess_byte_word_checkpoint.py` captured the actual **1,432-update /one-pass** reader snapshot through an already-open atomically published checkpoint inode. Its whole file is retained at `checkpoints/byte-word-reader-v1/intermediate-assessment.pt` with SHA `1204cbe35e761323ba934979be7c95b0394a578729c6340c0fab5d9d6b506a6d`. On the same256 earlier validation cases, it generated **2 exact transcripts proper /0 blank /1 wrong-image original-target matches**. Both proper successes were the digit `1`; one also matched with the wrong crop. Proper CER was0.9763 overall, so useful word reading remains unachieved. Case/target/counterpart matching, identical blank outputs, counterpart-equivalent raw transcripts and actual input pixels were checked; final eight-pass learning continues. Evidence: `reports/byte-word-reader-v1/intermediate-comparison.json` and `intermediate-correct-cases.json`.

The completed exposure audit scanned **4,139,183,022 source token positions** across older314M, warmup1.254B, current2.326B and queued245M code data, with **zero exact retained knowledge/math/public-code question/specification matches**. These streams overlap and include not-yet-trained planned data; the figure is not a unique-token/training-exposure count. Full SFT sequence scanning also completed with zero matches. `scripts/audit_preference_exposure.py` independently checked12,000 original chosen responses,12,000 rejected responses and12,000 truncated learner-visible PPO prompts, also finding zero exact matches. Benchmark/source bytes and encoder/scanner implementations were unchanged. Generated PPO rollouts, paraphrases, shared solutions and semantic overlap are not covered (`reports/original-feedback-benchmark-exposure-v1.json`).

`slm_lab.text_localization_data` prepared original word-box supervision on **5,000 existing full training photos /400 validation photos**, retaining **248,707/16,407 original word regions**. The **167,034/11,034 legible words** supply localization targets; unreadable regions are ignored for negative training decisions. Inputs contain complete photographs without transcripts or annotation boxes. Original polygons/IDs and photo attribution remain recorded, and all projected rectangles passed finite/positive/in-frame checks. Geometry JSONL was compressed losslessly with decoded-byte verification, saving82,507,251 bytes. These are the existing development splits and original-format mirrored TextOCR annotations, with the previously stated original-endpoint equivalence limitation. Dataset: `data/text-localization-v1/manifest.json`.

`slm_lab.word_localizer` implements an own **1,627,903-parameter scratch CNN** that predicts word centers and sizes from768px complete photos at three spatial levels, inspired by [Objects as Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/1904.07850). Collision fallbacks represent every legible train/validation box without omissions. A four-photo CUDA learning check produced finite gradients at935,997,952 peak allocated bytes; its temporary weights were discarded. The own policy plus both proposed detector and byte reader would total **709,755,680 parameters**, but neither component has been integrated into the policy.

`scripts/train_word_localizer.py` is **queued after the verified live byte-word reader's completed training and final assessment**. Its finite budget is ten passes/12,500 updates/50,000 full-photo exposures on existing GPU3, with a fresh scratch initialization and all photo-byte/pixel checks before learning. Fixed confidence0.3/NMS0.5 and a maximum1,000 predictions are declared before training. Before/after assessment covers **all400 full validation photos**, using one-to-one IoU0.5 matches and unreadable-region ignores, proper images, deterministic blank input and actual other-photo prediction controls. A separate explicitly oracle diagnostic reconstructed11,020/11,034 original boxes after fixed decoding; that is a geometry/postprocessing check, not learned detection. Eighteen focused geometry/loss/input/control checks passed. No trained detector or reliable full-photo reading is claimed. Plan/status: `reports/word-localizer-v1/`; readiness: `reports/word-localizer-preparation-readiness-v1.json`.

The complete implementation suite passed **356 tests in84.49s** using `.venv/bin/python -m pytest -q` (`reports/implementation-tests-v31.log`). The console-entrypoint attempt failed collection because the repository's `scripts` package was absent from its import path; the failure log is preserved, and the documented module invocation passed. These are software/integrity checks, not trained-model quality scores.

`slm_lab.photo_word_reading` composes the own scratch detector and byte reader using **complete-photo pixels only**. Every crop comes from a predicted box, with the same fixed5% horizontal/10% vertical margins used in real-word training preparation, followed by32x512 bicubic resizing. Neither annotated boxes, reference transcripts nor image questions enter inference. Primary whole-photo word credit requires both a one-to-one IoU0.5 match and an exact case-sensitive Unicode/whitespace-normalized transcript; location-only detections are scored separately. Missed words, duplicate detections, wrong transcripts and invalid UTF-8 cannot earn word credit.

`scripts/assess_photo_word_reading.py` is verified waiting on the exact live detector controller. After both own components complete their declared learning budgets, it verifies their checkpoint hashes and loaded source/runtime, then assesses **all400 full validation photos /11,034 original legible words** with proper/blank/counterpart controls. Actual detector boxes, full-photo bytes/pixels, case count and counterpart identity must agree with the detector's prior complete assessment. No target crop can substitute for a prediction. Original target binding covered all16,407 regions, and exact RGB recovery was checked on eight actual photos (`reports/photo-word-reading-source-input-check-v2.json`). Fourteen meaningful inference/matching/checkpoint/input/coverage checks passed, and the complete suite passed **370 tests in84.99s** (`reports/implementation-tests-v32.log`). This is a scheduled standalone composition assessment; native policy question answering, document order and reliable multimodal ability are not established. Status: `reports/photo-word-reading-v1/`.

The immutable **9,000-update** reader snapshot produced **165/256 exact proper transcripts**, versus2/256 blank and0/256 wrong-crop original-target matches. Real words scored **46/128 (35.94%)** with CER0.4228; rendered cases scored **119/128 (92.97%)** with CER0.0091. The proper successes now include ordinary multi-character words, rather than only the earlier digit1. All prior case/target/counterpart identities, actual pixel hashes, blank-output consistency and counterpart transcript equivalence were checked. This is the same reused crop-development set, with changed architecture/data/training budget from prior experiments, and final eight-pass training remains live. It is evidence of component image-conditioned reading, not whole-photo/native capability or architecture superiority. Reports: `reports/byte-word-reader-v1/later-comparison.json`, `later-image-dependence.json`; exact snapshot: `checkpoints/byte-word-reader-v1/later-assessment.pt` (SHA `7d7051524954c40d65ab0679dadf050cd5061ff421615c38354c3bd3d3d0281f`).

The completed unpromoted scene/QA rehearsal model is now preserved as an exact XOR archive against the retained own vision-stage anchor, saving another **524,370,964 payload bytes**. All original file bytes were reconstructed to SHA `ac1b299d95185be260aeb8d21d4cf9d734d9946de6cab08ffbb3c40ed5af1320` before removing the redundant full file. Keep `checkpoints/clevr-dense-learning-v1/vision/model.pt` and `checkpoints/clevr-joint-rehearsal-v1/joint/model.pt.xor/`; restore with the documented `slm_lab.checkpoint_xor restore` command before further use. No live model or required anchor was removed (`reports/clevr-joint-rehearsal-v1/exact-xor-archive-v1.json`).

The own byte reader completed its **11,456-update /732,680-example** eight-pass budget and final assessment. Both producer and DataLoader worker are terminal. Final unchanged256-case exactness is **168 proper /0 blank /0 wrong**, including **47/128 real words** and **121/128 rendered cases**. Its immutable inference model is `checkpoints/byte-word-reader-v1/model.pt`, SHA `3fbc9985748c51186f1d66e07764f4ab5ce1126eff96b1aec9b071db850bd187`. It remains a separate word-crop component, not a promoted native policy (`reports/byte-word-reader-v1/comparison.json`).

`slm_lab.full_byte_reading_evaluation` and `scripts/assess_all_byte_words.py` then inferred **every3,429 prepared validation crop**, using fixed batch32 and recording actual reader-input pixel hashes. Real words scored **478/1,429 (33.45%)**, CER0.4591, on397 original held-out photo groups, versus4 blank/3 wrong original-target matches. Rendered examples scored **1,887/2,000 (94.35%)**, CER0.0076, on1,860 text groups, versus0/0 controls. Original256 proper transcripts reproduced exactly despite batching. All words remain in the score; reused crops and repeated parents are not independent final benchmarks. Complete evidence: `reports/full-byte-reading-v1/summary.json`, `reading.json`. Real-scene recognition remains inadequate despite strong rendered-text performance.

The full-photo detector has now admitted all5,000 training/400 validation photos, passed its own four-photo CUDA gradient/readiness check, exactly restored scratch tensors and started **12,500 updates /50,000 full-photo exposures** on existing GPU3. The composed400-photo reading evaluator waits for its completion. Plan/status: `reports/word-localizer-v1/`. New whole-crop coverage/control/weighted-error checks passed, and the complete suite passed **373 tests in85.59s** (`reports/implementation-tests-v34.log`). A preceding full run had a distributed worker abort (372 passed); that isolated test and the subsequent complete suite passed without changing training/objective sources. Both failure and recheck logs are retained.

Real failure inspection found a blurry `1958` crop predicted as `1953`, and sideways `BRIDGESTONE` predicted as `S`. The stored crops are already enlarged, so stored height alone does not measure native source readability. Among all133 real validation crops whose height exceeds1.5 times width, unrotated reading scored31 correct; uniform90/270-degree rotations scored11/9. Allowing reference-informed choice among all three candidates gives46 correct, an explicitly **oracle upper-bound diagnostic**, not a deployable improvement. Rotation alone cannot explain/fix the951 real-word errors. No orientation selector was deployed, and all original scores remain unchanged (`reports/full-byte-reading-v1/real-aspect-strata.json`, `tall-orientation-diagnostic.json`).

The scratch full-photo detector completed **12,500 updates /50,000 actual photo exposures**. On all400 original validation photos it matched **6,391/11,034 legible word boxes**, with precision50.11%,recall57.92% andF1.5373 under the fixed IoU/ignore protocol. Its immutable model SHA is `ff15d95a48f753b936afcfba5d1f5de129fe9c4ae15ce710f4821b0712568294` (`reports/word-localizer-v1/comparison.json`). This remains a development assessment of axis-aligned localization.

The actual complete-photo detector+reader assessment also finished. **2,127/11,034 words were both located and exactly transcribed**, with precision16.73%,recall19.28%,F1.1791; blank and wrong-photo exact-word matches were both zero. Many false/missed detections and incorrect transcripts remain. Every actual box/case/input/counterpart matched the prior localization assessment, and no target boxes/transcripts entered inference. This demonstrates limited full-photo word reading by the own standalone components, not reliable/native multimodal question answering (`reports/photo-word-reading-v1/reading.json`).

The queued expanded-feedback controller was found genuinely terminal (tool exit143, actualPID302301 absent) while its last state was waiting. No reward/DPO/PPO stage or training plan existed. The unchanged script and the same capability/real-reading-guarded arguments were verified and restored in a detached process; main and SFT training continued. Old status and restoration evidence are preserved in `reports/extended-human-feedback-v1/terminated-idle-controller-restoration-v1.json`. It remains waiting for the selected joint SFT policy, rather than newly completed feedback training.

`slm_lab.more_real_words` prepared **121,591 unused original human word targets**, including3,875 non-ASCII strings, from3,164 of the existing5,000 licensed training-photo parents. It references original cached JPEGs instead of downloading/copying images, rehashes all5,000 native sources, uses original boxes with5%/10% margins and native crop sides of at least8px, then performs one32x512 bicubic resize. All248,707 original annotations are accounted for: prior41,585 exposed real words,81,673 unreadable labels,2,294 protected rendered-validation words,1,378 sub-8px crops,174 small original boxes,12 byte-budget exclusions and121,591 additions. No validation image/text split or old benchmark score changed. Mirror equivalence and native readability limitations remain explicit (`data/more-native-real-words-v1/manifest.json`).

`slm_lab.real_word_refinement` loads **all91,585 previous training words plus121,591 additions** (213,176 rows), keeps all3,429 validation crops, and shuffles chunks of at most8 words while consuming every row once per pass. Hash-bound decoded-frame reuse is limited to192MiB per worker. `scripts/train_native_word_refinement.py` is running **four passes /13,324 updates /852,704 word exposures** from the completed own reader atLR2e-4 on existing GPU2. It adds no parameters, uses no external weights/teacher labels, and retains both completed original component checkpoints. A four-native/four-previous-crop CUDA update had finite gradients at258,286,592 peak allocated bytes; temporary updates were restored exactly before learning. Final evaluation repeats both original256 and complete3,429 cases, documents lost correct words and does not automatically promote the candidate. Native or whole-photo gains still require separate assessment. Plan/status: `reports/native-word-refinement-v1/`.

Seven focused new sampler/geometry/cache/source checks passed. The complete implementation suite passed **380 tests in85.66s** (`reports/implementation-tests-v35.log`); these are implementation evidence, not reliable broad capability or high-end model parity.

`slm_lab.refined_photo_evaluation` and the detached `scripts/assess_refined_photo_reading.py` now schedule a **matched400-photo assessment** after the exact live reader-refinement controller completes all13,324 updates and both crop assessments. The original detector model, photos, predicted boxes, normalized word-crop pixels, crop transform, code/runtime, target population and proper/blank/counterpart scoring must match the completed baseline. Only the own reader weights change. The comparison records gained original word targets and previously correct targets lost, rather than only a net score. It adds no training, silently excluded cases or automatic policy promotion. Six meaningful comparison checks and the full **386-test suite (84.06s)** passed (`reports/refined-photo-reading-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v36.log`). Status/plan: `reports/refined-photo-reading-v1/`. Actual refined full-photo results and native-policy integration remain pending.

Native-word refinement and its matched full-photo assessment have now completed. All **13,324 updates /852,704 actual word exposures** match the four-pass budget. Real-crop accuracy rose **478→546/1,429 (33.45%→38.21%)**, CER0.4591→0.4196, while rendered accuracy decreased **1,887→1,876/2,000 (94.35%→93.80%)** and68 previous correct crops were lost. One real prediction was invalid UTF-8. The original256 subset improved168→170 exact; this does not hide losses in the larger cohort. Candidate model SHA: `7e6850f88ccbe55794dd4defb1359e04a8473c014f48bf7c2708cc5b33938110` (`reports/native-word-refinement-v1/comparison.json`).

With the exact same detector/crops on all400 complete photos, correctly located-and-transcribed words rose **2,127→2,454/11,034**. Recall rose19.28%→22.24%,precision16.73%→19.30%,F1.1791→.2067. The target-level comparison found **458 gained /131 previously correct lost**; blank/wrong exact-word matches remain zero. False positives/misses remain frequent, and this reused development result does not establish reliable generic or native policy capability. Both own readers remain preserved; no candidate was automatically promoted (`reports/refined-photo-reading-v1/comparison.json`, `reading.json`).

`slm_lab.own_ocr_context` prepares an optional **native-model conditioning interface**, limited to the original question and our own pixel-predicted text/boxes/confidence. It whitelists prediction fields, drops invalid/empty strings, selects at most64 predicted words, removes low-confidence context until the complete question/BOS/declared generation budget fit, and escapes literal angle-bracket tokenizer markers inside JSON. It never accepts reference-answer or image-class parameters. This is interface preparation only: the language policy has not yet been tested/trained with it. Five budget/metadata/Unicode/control-marker checks and the complete **391-test suite (84.39s)** passed (`reports/own-ocr-context-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v37.log`).

`slm_lab.own_ocr_question_evaluation` and `scripts/assess_own_ocr_questions.py` are now running an actual **128-question native-policy experiment** on the original unique real-photo cases. The own vision-stage policy is frozen atSHA `827bd667ed5552d7cbbe6a45578f1d8628dae6a19c17ab066a5b7d592e53273c`; own detector/refined-reader weights are also unchanged. It compares **plain questions /empty context schema /own pixel-predicted text context**, each with proper/normalized-gray-blank/original-counterpart controls and greedy32-token answers. Both the448px policy image and768px own reading input derive from the actual credited photo. Each changed-image control gets its own OCR predictions; proper text is not reused as a hint. Original human source question/ten-answer/consensus identity, prior native prompt and actual policy pixel hashes are checked. Every original128 case is retained, and only question/prediction fields enter generation. New native answer performance is pending; this untrained-context frozen-policy diagnostic does not replace the longer queued main curriculum or demonstrate generic/frontier skill. Progress/results: `reports/own-ocr-questions-v1/`. Seven focused context/control/metric checks and all **393 tests in83.01s** passed (`reports/own-ocr-native-question-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v38.log`).

That frozen-policy experiment completed with **0/128 exact answers and zero ten-answer agreement in every mode/control**. Fresh plain generations exactly reproduced the original baseline. A post-generation diagnostic found the expected answer already present as one own-predicted context word in30 cases, but the policy still produced no correct answer; many outputs repeat its earlier rendered-scene description format or malformed numeric/context continuations. Answer presence is a label-informed diagnostic, not a deployable selector or proof that the full question is answerable from the supplied text. Thus measured word recognition did not transfer to native question answering in this untrained-context policy. No integration was promoted. Original human-question instruction/context training and broader main curriculum remain necessary, with new measured preservation/independent capability evidence (`reports/own-ocr-questions-v1/summary.json`, `answer-support-diagnostic.json`).

`slm_lab.own_ocr_training_data` completed original human reading supervision with **both plain and own-predicted-context inputs per question**. It retains all 5,858 training/468 validation original questions and unchanged human answers, producing 11,716/936 input variants with explicit doubled target exposure. Before preparing, it required the completed original question/photo audit and rehashed all 4,608 actual credited photos. Own completed detector/refined-reader predictions were generated once per photo; neither reference answers nor original OCR/class labels enter prediction or context selection. No question is filtered because its answer is missing from the predictions. Context uses at most 64 valid predicted words, four-decimal geometry/confidence and fixed 256-token headroom independent of target length, while actual own-encoder checks prohibit question/answer truncation. No images or model weights were downloaded. Dataset: `data/own-ocr-human-questions-v1/manifest.json`; preparation log: `reports/own-ocr-human-question-preparation-v1.log`.

`slm_lab.own_ocr_question_admission` reconstructed every training/validation prompt and complete target from the original human question and recorded own predictions. All variant identities, compressed/decoded file hashes, token exposures, component/runtime hashes and photo bytes passed. The joint schedule now retains the original 5,858 plain questions and adds only their 5,858 own-context training variants; validation variants never enter training. Its actual composition preview contains **262,606 source rows / 16,413 joint updates**, including every prior code/math/image/dialogue record, 78,255 additional photos, 23,847 additional words, original CLEVR questions and public exercise rehearsal. The waiting controller was replaced only after confirming its identity and absence of child jobs; the live main training process and feedback controller were preserved. Receipts: `reports/own-context-human-question-full-reconstruction-v1.json`, `reports/dialogue-retention-v1/own-context-data-composition-preview-v1.json`, `own-context-admission-reload-v1.json`. Training on these variants remains queued.

`slm_lab.own_context_transfer_evaluation` now schedules matched 128-question assessments before/after joint training using the exact compact training interface, fixed own components, original full-photo pixels and proper/blank/counterpart contexts. It rejects any lost previously correct answer, changed prediction/prompt/pixel identity or inconsistent generated-answer score, alongside all existing capability, module, visual reasoning, plain reading and human-preference guards. A two-question runtime check used the actual own frozen policy and all three controls; it scored **0/2**, verifying mechanics without a capability claim (`reports/own-context-transfer-two-case-runtime-v1.json`). Seventeen focused checks and the complete **410-test suite in 81.68s** passed (`reports/own-context-handoff-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v40.log`). Conditioned-policy gains and independent generic visual capability remain unverified.

The queued human-feedback controller now also uses `--own-context-reading-guard`. Both DPO and any reward-gated PPO branch must preserve compact own-reader answers on all **128 original validation photos**, in addition to the existing nine capability families, plain reading and offline human-pair checks. It reuses the selected SFT policy's matched report only when model, dataset, own components and evaluator/runtime identities agree; otherwise it evaluates that exact base before feedback learning. Each branch receives the same fixed reading protocol, and losing one previously correct answer withholds selection even if another gain preserves total accuracy. Passing this check cannot override another failed guard. The previous idle controller's source/status was preserved before verified replacement; main language learning and the joint controller were unchanged (`reports/extended-human-feedback-v1/own-context-retention-reload-v1.json`). Twenty-three focused checks and all **419 tests in 84.32s** passed (`reports/feedback-own-context-guard-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v41.log`). Feedback learning remains queued; no conditioned-policy improvement or fresh human evaluation is claimed.

The recent **7,000-update** language snapshot was assessed against the exact completed 4,785-update warmup with the unchanged evaluator/runtime, sampled token windows and generated-answer budgets (`reports/recent-foundation-7000-v1/comparison.json`). The current stage's actual exposure ledger contains **1,834,560,000 targets**, and all 112 frozen vision tensors remain exact. Held code NLL improved **0.866 → 0.815**, Wikipedia **3.052 → 2.741**. Completion syntax rose **3 → 8/24**, instruction syntax **0 → 2/24**, while functional/efficiency passes stayed **0/24 in both modes**; all syntax passes were Python, with JavaScript still zero. On all 559 reserved questions, generated accuracy rose **15 → 25 correct (2.68% → 4.47%)**, gaining 16 and losing six earlier correct answers. Format compliance rose 16.28% → 36.49%, while forced-choice accuracy fell **13.42% → 11.81%** (95% interval 9.39–14.75%, including 11.18% mean chance). This remains weak development evidence, before the queued instruction/image/joint/feedback stages. The assessment held the periodic snapshot open read-only, copied no model bytes and released its temporary alias afterward; the original snapshot may later rotate away. Main learning continued, and no policy was promoted.

`scripts/fetch_nq_open_source.py` retained the original [Google NQ-Open release](https://github.com/google-research-datasets/natural-questions/tree/master/nq_open) at revision `fb26a3073b1fe636c97302890a27b491d6530130`, checking each downloaded file against its publisher Git blob and SHA256. The publisher explicitly assigns **CC BY-SA 3.0 to the data**, separately from the repository code's Apache-2.0 license. All **87,925 train / 3,610 original development questions** have complete reference strings and zero exact canonical question overlap (`data/nq-open-original-source-v1/manifest.json`). This uses human-annotation-derived questions/answers from the publisher, with no model teacher or external weights.

`slm_lab.nq_open_data` prepared **87,925 brief-answer training instructions / 3,610 validation records**, keeping every original answer alias and using the unchanged first supplied string as the training target. Original source rows, line numbers, question identities, license/citation information, own tokenizer/config and full sequence target counts are retained. Actual targets per pass are **434,256 train / 18,031 validation**, with no truncated question or answer. Official development and existing exact reserved knowledge/math/code questions are protected from the new training source. This adds original human fact-question supervision beyond source-document likelihood; it has **not been admitted or trained**. Prior/queued training exposure to these new development questions, semantic overlap and article grouping still require audit; the open files omit article identity. Preparation is not expert coverage or independent final evaluation. Source/processed files occupy about 125 MiB. Four targeted alias/target/input/split checks and all **423 tests in 85.78s** passed (`reports/nq-open-data-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v42.log`).

The independent `slm_lab.nq_input_audit` verified all **91,535 original question/answer/alias rows**, including complete own-encoder input IDs, response-only label masks, EOS, split/group identities and recorded target exposures (`reports/nq-original-input-audit-v1.json`). `scripts/audit_nq_exposure.py` is scanning all four frozen past/current/queued packed corpora, the prior and queued supervised/image/caption data, the new QA data, and human-feedback chosen/rejected/PPO prompt inputs. The first older-corpus hit is confirmed by an independent direct tokenizer decode: the original development question about the 2015 Rugby World Cup appears in actual past training text (`reports/nq-development-exposure-first-actual-match-v1.json`). Thus exact official train/dev separation alone does not make all development questions unseen. The complete exposure scan remains active; its findings are not yet final (`reports/nq-development-exposure-v1/`).

The idle joint controller now queues the **87,925 original training rows subject to both completed audits**, while retaining every prior rehearsal input. Its actual source-count preview is **350,531 rows / 21,909 updates** (`reports/dialogue-retention-v1/human-fact-qa-composition-preview-v1.json`). A current admission attempt correctly rejects the missing completed exposure report; no new QA training has started. `slm_lab.nq_open_evaluation` schedules full **3,610-question native closed-book** before/after assessments using unchanged original answer aliases and a 32-token generation budget. Full original metrics and the potential-exact-exposure/no-exact-match groups remain separate; absence of an exact match does not certify semantic/article independence. The added per-case retention guard cannot hide a lost correct answer behind an aggregate gain, and all earlier capability/module/image/reading/preference gates remain intact. Selected-policy reports preserve the matched QA assessment for subsequent review. Reload evidence: `reports/dialogue-retention-v1/human-fact-qa-admission-reload-v1.json`. Sixteen targeted input/native-metric checks and the complete **435-test suite in 86.63s** passed (`reports/nq-source-native-evaluation-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v43.log`). Native QA gains remain unmeasured pending actual admitted training.

The exposure audit subsequently completed across **4,139,183,022 packed token positions / 1,557,290 encoder-visible source examples**, including potential future inputs and overlapping/repeated streams. It flagged **18 of 3,610 original development questions**: three in older/queued sources and fifteen as full-question substrings inside longer original NQ training questions. All eighteen findings were independently confirmed by directly decoding actual packed token windows or actual encoder input IDs (`reports/nq-complete-exposure-findings-independent-verification-v1.json`). All original benchmark cases remain; **3,592** have no exact match in the declared scanned inputs, which still does not prove answer/concept/article/paraphrase independence. The real `nq_input_audit.admit` call now passes after rechecking input/runtime/source hashes and every packed training shard, returning only the 87,925 training rows (`reports/nq-training-admission-readiness-v1.json`). The prepared manifest's original `training_admitted=false` describes preparation time; current readiness is recorded separately. No new QA learning has begun.

`slm_lab.feedback_nq_guard` and the queued `--human-fact-question-guard` extend DPO/gated-PPO selection with matched native factual answers on all **3,610** cases. The exact selected SFT policy's report is reused only when model, inputs, exposure evidence and evaluator/runtime agree; otherwise it is freshly assessed before feedback training. Every branch is evaluated with unchanged aliases, generation budget and exposure groups. Losing a previously correct answer withholds selection even if total accuracy is unchanged; passing factual QA cannot override an earlier code, image-reading or preference failure. All prior training/evaluation/selection calls are preserved. The old idle feedback controller's source/status were saved before verified detached replacement (`reports/extended-human-feedback-v1/human-fact-question-guard-reload-v1.json`). Thirty-one focused checks and all **443 tests in 85.77s** passed (`reports/feedback-factual-qa-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v44.log`). Main learning and the joint controller remain live; feedback learning and all new capability results are still pending.

Complete original JavaScript publisher suites are now executable under a separately pinned Node 20.20.2 / Jest 30.3.0 runtime. Every one of the **37 held module references** passes all **605 case records / 705 assertions**, with no skipped cases; source files, interfaces, original test bytes, dependency files and runtime are hashed (`reports/exercism-javascript-publisher-references-v1.json`). Eight actual execution probes verify blocked outside-file reads, child-process creation, sockets and test writes, plus rejection of wrong outputs, premature exit, invalid syntax and excessive resident memory (`reports/javascript-kernel-and-report-verification-v1.json`). Same-process Jest reports are not adversarial score-forgery proof, and original suites do not establish all-JavaScript correctness or optimal complexity. The dependency directory currently resides on the temporary filesystem and must be restored exactly if removed.

The idle joint controller now requires matched full-module JavaScript assessments before and after learning, beside its existing Python/C++, image/reading, factual-QA and preference criteria. Selection rejects any lost previously correct JavaScript module or lower delivery/syntax/pass rate and preserves the selected report for later review. All **19 existing run-call arguments and six existing exercise-assessment rows** remain exact; training inputs are unchanged. Only the verified waiting controller was replaced, preserving live main learning and the feedback controller (`reports/dialogue-retention-v1/javascript-module-guard-reload-v1.json`). Eight focused checks and all **451 implementation tests in 80.84s** pass (`reports/exercise-js-module-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v45.log`). The actual own completed 300-update six-language prototype now scores **16/37 complete file responses, 8/37 parsable modules and 0/37 complete publisher-suite passes**. One parsable module lacks a required export; all seven remaining modules fail behavior cases. A generated pangram implementation tests whether text is empty, and a sum-of-multiples implementation substitutes a one-argument numeric lookup for the declared interface. This is a separate earlier prototype, not a score for the ongoing main foundation (`reports/exercism-javascript-own-policy-baseline-v1.json`, `exercism-javascript-own-policy-diagnostic-v1.json`). No policy is promoted; generic/all-code correctness and high-end capability remain unverified.

The queued feedback controller now also requires `--javascript-module-guard` for DPO and any reward-gated PPO branch. It will assess all **37 original JavaScript modules**, reuse the selected SFT report only when policy/source/toolchain/runtime identities match, and require the full original test inventory for successful modules. Each branch must preserve previous correct modules and delivery/syntax/pass rates; passing JavaScript cannot override another failed capability, reading, factual-QA or offline-preference check. All prior reward/DPO/PPO training calls and arguments, evaluations, cleanup calls and retention checks remain exact. The verified idle feedback controller was replaced while main and joint controllers continued (`reports/extended-human-feedback-v1/javascript-module-guard-reload-v1.json`). Twenty-four focused checks and all **467 implementation tests in 87.31s** pass (`reports/feedback-javascript-module-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v46.log`). Actual feedback learning and future-branch native assessments remain queued; no new functional-code gain or high-end equivalence is claimed.

The complete upcoming code-instruction input audit read all **906,952 frozen rows through the actual CPU Encoder**. All **897,452 CodeSearchNet code rows** preserve complete prompts and responses; two of the 9,500 general human instructions have clipped responses, with one also losing prompt tokens. Every BOS/EOS and response-only label mask agrees with the original encoded inputs. Actual one-pass loss targets are **262,177,794** (`reports/code-instruction-input-audit-v1.json`); final shuffled-batch remainder repeats still require training receipts. This is input integrity, not algorithmic validity.

A separate pinned Babel 7.29.9 parser checked all **92,606 JavaScript instruction rows**. It recognized 92,589 outer interfaces, left 17 unsupported source/context shapes explicit, and found **982 parameter headers cut by the first-brace transform** (`reports/javascript-instruction-interface-audit-v1.json`). Examples include `options = {}` and destructured object parameters. All 982 corrected training variants retain the exact original code targets and source metadata, replace only the incomplete header with the full parsed source header, and pass independent parent reconstruction plus complete actual-Encoder input/label checks. They contain **429,998 targets per pass**, with maximum complete input length **2,799 tokens** (`data/complete-js-parameters-v1/manifest.json`, `reports/complete-js-parameter-variant-reconstruction-v1.json`). The variants are prepared, **not yet admitted or trained**; frozen live-stage data and controller sources remain unchanged. Ruby signature contexts span multiple source lines in 31,704 rows; that is a source-format indicator and not a parsed universal proof of invalid interfaces. No new learned capability is claimed.

The 982 complete-parameter variants now pass live training admission with fresh parsing of every original source header, exact target/metadata reconstruction, complete actual Encoder sequences and zero overlap with original validation file groups (`reports/complete-js-parameter-admission-readiness-v2.json`). The waiting joint controller appends them beside all **350,531 prior rows**, producing **351,513 source rows / 21,970 planned updates**; prior training/evaluation calls and all native/capability/preference guards remain exact. The source/tokenization/parser identities are bound and rechecked before learning. Main and feedback controllers were preserved during verified idle replacement (`reports/dialogue-retention-v1/complete-js-parameter-admission-reload-v1.json`, `complete-js-parameter-composition-preview-v1.json`). Ten targeted checks and all **477 implementation tests in 86.75s** pass (`reports/complete-js-parameter-admission-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v47.log`). Admission is readiness and scheduling: no training on these variants or measured coding gain has occurred. The original preparation manifests remain unchanged as historical records; current admission evidence is separate.

Syntax-aware Go/Ruby source auditing used isolated, publisher-wheel-hash-verified Tree-sitter 0.26.0 / Go grammar 0.25.0 / Ruby grammar 0.23.1 tools; the training environment was preserved. The complete 906,952-row scan parsed all 200,000 Go interfaces and 35,685/35,691 Ruby interfaces. It found **11,435 incomplete Go headers**, including 8,953 incomplete parameter lists, and **35,571 Ruby prompts requiring header correction**; 31,594 Ruby prompts include method-body text in their signature context. Six unsupported Ruby source/context shapes remain explicit (`reports/goruby-instruction-header-audit-v1.json`). Twelve actual byte-range/grammar probes plus incomplete-source rejection pass (`reports/goruby-header-parser-probes-v1.json`). The official [Python parser documentation](https://tree-sitter.github.io/py-tree-sitter/) and [Go](https://github.com/tree-sitter/tree-sitter-go/tree/v0.25.0)/[Ruby](https://github.com/tree-sitter/tree-sitter-ruby/tree/v0.23.1) grammar sources describe the parsing tools; none are language-model weights.

All **47,006 source variants** retain original code and metadata, restore complete parsed headers and fit complete own-Encoder inputs, with **10,523,506 targets per source pass / 2,981 maximum input tokens** (`data/complete-goruby-headers-v1/manifest.json`). Fresh original-parent/header/target/mask reconstruction and zero original validation-file-group overlap pass (`reports/complete-goruby-header-admission-readiness-v1.json`). The verified idle joint controller now appends them while preserving all **351,513 prior rows**, yielding **398,519 source rows / 24,908 planned updates**. All 19 previous run-call arguments and earlier selection checks remain exact; main and feedback controllers were preserved (`reports/dialogue-retention-v1/goruby-header-admission-reload-v1.json`, `goruby-header-composition-preview-v1.json`). Eight targeted admission checks and all **485 implementation tests in 86.71s** pass (`reports/goruby-header-admission-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v48.log`). New-variant learning remains queued. Original code algorithms/dependencies, native Go/Ruby behavior and high-end equivalence are not established by source parsing or these implementation checks.

Full original Go publisher module evaluation is now available with the exact publisher-checksummed **Go 1.27.2 SDK**, an isolated shared compile cache and trusted event converter; SDK files/tool identity are hashed (`.tools/go-toolchain-v1.json`). All **42 held original reference modules** pass their complete original tests and `go.mod` files, covering **827 test/subtest case records**, with no benchmarks or asymptotic-optimality claim (`reports/exercism-go-publisher-references-v1.json`). The first 41/42 diagnostic was preserved: one publisher config omitted its `cases_test.go` helper. The completed runner now retains all direct original test files from the pinned archive, without dropping a failing exercise (`reports/exercism-go-missing-helper-diagnostic-v1.json`).

Nine actual compiled probes verify blocked outside reads, child processes, sockets, original test writes and other-process signals, plus rejection of wrong outputs, early successful exit, invalid source and resident-memory excess (`reports/go-kernel-and-report-verification-v1.json`). Compilation and runtime are restricted before process startup; runtime permits Go threads and self-thread preemption signals, with CPU/address/RSS/wall limits. Same-process Go event reports remain unsuitable as adversarial score-forgery proof. The temporary SDK/cache must be restored with exact identities if removed. The official [Go installation source](https://go.dev/doc/install) and [test command documentation](https://pkg.go.dev/cmd/go#hdr-Test_packages) describe the tooling; these reference/kernel results do not measure model capability.

The verified idle joint controller now schedules matching full42 Go-module generation/compilation/publisher-suite assessments before and after learning, beside its existing Python/C++/JavaScript and broader image/factual/preference checks. Any lost correct Go module or lower delivery/compilation/pass rate withholds selection; the selected native report is retained. All 19 prior run-call arguments and eight prior exercise-assessment rows remain exact, with training sources unchanged (`reports/dialogue-retention-v1/native-go-module-guard-reload-v1.json`). Eight focused checks and all **493 implementation tests in 83.26s** pass (`reports/exercise-go-module-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v49.log`). The actual completed six-language300-update own prototype now scores **12/42 complete named-file responses, 0/42 compiled modules and 0/42 full publisher-suite passes** (`reports/exercism-go-own-policy-baseline-v1.json`, `exercism-go-own-policy-diagnostic-v1.json`). Typical failures include repeated function declarations, missing imports/variables and wrong parameter counts. This is a separate earlier prototype, not a score for the ongoing main foundation. No policy is promoted.

The queued feedback controller now also uses `--go-module-guard`. Both DPO and any reward-gated PPO branch will be assessed on all **42 original native Go modules**, matching policy/source/test/module/toolchain/runtime identities. A selected SFT report is reused only when those identities and full coverage agree; otherwise the exact base is evaluated before reward/DPO learning. Selection rejects any lost correct module or lower delivery/compilation/pass rate, and successful reports must include all original passing tests/subtests without skips. A Go pass cannot override earlier capability, plain/own-reader, factual-QA, JavaScript or offline-preference failure. All prior training/evaluation/cleanup calls and arguments remain exact; the verified idle feedback source/status was preserved before detached replacement (`reports/extended-human-feedback-v1/native-go-module-guard-reload-v1.json`). Twenty-four focused checks and all **509 implementation tests in 86.07s** pass (`reports/feedback-go-module-focused-tests-v1.log`, `reports/implementation-tests-v50.log`). Main training, joint controller and the actual prototype Go assessment remain live; feedback learning and future native branch results are still queued.

The live original-photo VQA collector reached **718 committed photos** and correctly waited when workspace free space fell below its 35 GiB reserve. An inactive completed legacy one-update SFT checkpoint is now stored as an exact compressed difference against the retained `checkpoints/full-first-pretrain/model.pt` anchor. Whole-file restoration SHA256 verifies every original byte before removal, reclaiming **860,766,216 bytes**; the original legacy report was saved before its missing planned-step field was truthfully filled from the exact checkpoint metadata. Current models, active training and every prior archive anchor are preserved. The same live collector resumed original requests and reached at least **728 photos** (`reports/vqa-collection-storage-unblock-v1.json`). Source pacing and publisher cooldowns remain enforced. The paired dataset is still collecting, with no finalized manifest or new visual capability claim. Restore the historical model with `.venv/bin/python -m slm_lab.checkpoint_xor restore --archive checkpoints/full-first-sft/model.pt.xor --output checkpoints/full-first-sft/model.pt`; keep its full pretraining anchor.

FashionMNIST contributes only **500 of 56,237 examples (0.89%)** in the frozen main image stage. That stage also includes **20,176 everyday-photo examples, 23,831 scene-text examples, 1,382 document-page examples, 348 scientific images and 10,000 rendered-text examples**. The later joint stage retains that prior training and adds **78,255 human-captioned photos**, more real-word crops, original human reading questions and visual reasoning. Examples and crops are not counts of independent photographs. The main controller is currently in language pretraining; these additional image stages have not started.

Original VQA2 paired real-photo questions are now connected to the later joint and feedback stages. Admission requires the collector's final manifest, original source hashes, complete question/answer/photo reconstruction, current stored creator declarations, image pixels, own-tokenizer response masks and distinct training/validation photo identities. A read-only preview verified **92 complete training candidate pairs and 11 validation candidate pairs**, reconstructing **206 original questions on 181 used photos** from a 784-photo journal snapshot. This preview does not finalize the collection, certify its final split screening or admit rows to training (`reports/vqa-pair-admission-preview-v1.json`).

Matched before/after native assessments use the frozen evaluator's exact finalized selection, up to128 pairs, with correct-photo, blank-photo and paired-counterpart controls. Selection retains previously correct answers and both-answers-correct pair accuracy; reports must include every selected case and consistent control/human-answer scores. The selected report is carried into both future DPO and any reward-gated PPO assessment. All previous learning/evaluation/cleanup calls and guards remain intact. Both waiting controllers were verified idle, snapshotted and reloaded; active main training and collection retained their identities (`reports/original-photo-pair-handoff-implementation-audit-v1.json`, `reports/dialogue-retention-v1/original-photo-pair-guard-reload-v1.json`, `reports/extended-human-feedback-v1/original-photo-pair-guard-reload-v1.json`). **557 implementation tests passed in87.17s** (`reports/implementation-tests-v51.log`). The original pair collector is still running; no new paired-photo learning or reliable generic-image capability is claimed.

The requested fresh **10,000-update** foundation assessment completed while the same main run continued. Compared with the completed warmup, held code prediction NLL fell **0.866→0.787**, and Wikipedia NLL **3.052→2.659** on the same64 windows/262,080 targets per component. Completion syntax remains **8/24**, with **0/24 functional and efficiency passes**; instruction syntax fell from2/24 at7000 to0/24 at10000. The 559-question generated-choice quiz scored **22 correct**, compared with15 after warmup and25 at7000. Forced-choice likelihood accuracy is12.88%, with an interval containing11.18% chance. The full trajectory also records the pre-warmup9/24 completion-syntax and22/559 quiz scores, so these are not presented as monotonic capability gains. Evidence: `reports/performance-evidence-2026-10-14-v1.json`, `reports/recent-foundation-10000-v1/comparison.json`. Separate reader gains lost32 earlier correct real-word crops and36 rendered crops; the five earlier correct photo answers are all “yes,” below the8/128 answer-mode baseline.

Public code variety now adds **11,797 original files from16 pinned repositories**: Linux, FreeRTOS, Zephyr, littlefs, lwIP, libpcap, tcpdump, Zeek, Suricata, OpenSSL, libssh2, libsodium, osquery, libseccomp, YARA and AFL++. Domains cover operating systems, embedded filesystems, packet/network protocols, cryptography, monitoring, detection and fuzzing. Recorded file SPDX expressions override recorded repository declarations; original license text, commit, source-file hash and archive hashes are preserved. Source code is treated as data. Initial parser/owner issues were inspected and the completed source populations were rejoined to original archives before resuming, rather than replaced. Data: `data/public-systems-variety-v1/manifest.json` (owned temporary storage through a workspace alias).

Every complete source file and EOS was re-encoded with our own tokenizer and matched against all actual packed tokens: **52,569,340 new training tokens /6,344,800 validation tokens**. The new mixture preserves all **245,123,134 earlier code training tokens** as explicit rehearsal. The verified idle joint controller now schedules a separate **1,136-update**, four-L40, language-only continuation before its existing real-word/joint stages. Original training/evaluation/cleanup calls, source rows and all earlier guards remain intact; main training, feedback and photo collection retained their identities. Admission/reload: `reports/systems-variety-training-admission-readiness-v1.json`, `reports/dialogue-retention-v1/systems-variety-code-reload-v1.json`. **599 implementation tests passed in81.78s**. New source learning is queued, not completed.

`slm_lab.recurrent_model` is a separate shared-depth architecture prototype inspired by [recurrent-depth latent reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05171). It keeps24 unique decoder layers, repeats the middle12 layers up to four times, and gives each extra core pass a learned residual gate. The model has **706,012,675 total parameters**, only three more than the own base, while its deepest path performs60 layer executions. Zero gates preserve all own base outputs; independent caches per visit preserve causal decoding. Twenty-five focused image/text/gradient/cache checks passed. More passes require more computation and cache memory.

A real CUDA experiment loaded our own completed warmup and learned only those three gates on32 original new-code training windows for64 updates/32,704 targets. Held prediction loss across16 separate validation windows changed **1.999011→1.998782**, about0.0115%; the one-pass frozen backbone remained exact. Peak allocated GPU memory was3,250,044,928 bytes. This small adapter feasibility result is not meaningful working-code improvement, full recurrent-backbone training or frontier parity; no policy was promoted (`reports/recurrent-owned-code-probe-v1/result.json`).

The new Rust native backend now compiles every original held reference and passes **24/24 original integration suites /355 registered cases**, including ignored and expected-panic cases, using a pinned Rust1.99 compiler/musl target and pinned original dependency sources (`reports/exercism-rust-publisher-references-v2.json`). The earlier8-second limit rejected one original reference; its23/24 diagnostic is retained. The calibrated protocol uses30 CPU seconds/45 wall seconds, without removing any cases. Fifteen parser/inventory checks pass. This verifies the reference backend; own-model Rust generation, kernel probes and selection integration remain pending, and no Rust coding gain is claimed.

The Rust backend subsequently passed **11 actual compiled kernel/report probes**, covering outside-file reads, child processes, sockets, original-test writes, allowed native threads, wrong outputs, early successful exits, invalid source, address-space exhaustion, CPU termination and the wall deadline (`reports/rust-kernel-and-report-verification-v1.json`). A separate evaluator generates all 24 original held modules, then compiles and executes complete reference-matched test inventories; 25 focused runner/comparison checks pass.

The actual earlier 300-update own six-language prototype assessment completed: **4/24 complete file deliveries, 0/24 compilations and 0/24 full publisher-suite passes**. Nineteen responses fail complete file delivery; one omits the required original Cargo target. Delivered programs still contain repeated definitions, missing methods, invalid expressions and wrong return types. The full source, native diagnostics and exact protocol remain in `reports/exercism-rust-owned-prototype-v1.json`; its terminal actor, policy/runtime hashes, complete row population and aggregate/schema checks were verified in `reports/exercism-rust-owned-prototype-verification-v1.json`. This prototype is distinct from the live main and recurrent policies. No Rust gain or future joint/feedback Rust selection integration is claimed.

The full language recurrence comparison completed on unique original systems-code windows. All branches began at the same own 4,785-update warmup and used the same 64 validation windows. The starting NLL was 1.838280. Ordinary training reached **1.688606 after 256 updates** and **1.615256 after 640 updates**; four-pass recurrence reached **1.688485 after 256 updates**. The 640-update control matches decoder-layer/token executions, a proxy rather than exact total FLOPs or wall time. All branches still passed **0/24 functional and efficiency tasks**; completion syntax was 9/24 initially, 2/24 after ordinary 256, 6/24 after ordinary 640 and 2/24 after recurrence. This trial uses 256 output tokens, distinct from the main assessment's 512-token protocol.

The recurrent branch's extra passes slightly increased loss relative to its own one-pass output (1.688458→1.688485), and its three raw gates stayed near 0.0035. This bounded single-seed trial does not demonstrate a benefit from the current gated repeat. Original vision and critic tensors remain exact, without establishing retained image behavior. The actor is terminal; all three checkpoint files were rehashed and are retained through `checkpoints/recurrent-full-code-ablation-v1`, with no policy promotion (`reports/recurrent-full-code-ablation-summary-v1.json`). Training took 66.35 seconds for ordinary 256, 165.26 seconds for ordinary 640 and 111.60 seconds for recurrent 256 on shared GPU3; the recurrent peak allocation was recorded in the final report. All **630 implementation tests passed in 101.71 seconds** (`reports/implementation-tests-v53.log`).

`ConditionedRecurrentLM` is a separate architecture candidate that combines the current state with the original prelude features through a learned adapter on every pass. Each shared-core attention/feed-forward residual receives an additional RMS normalization; there is no scalar pass-bypass gate. It retains all original 24 unique decoder layers and has **710,769,664 total parameters**, adding 4,756,992 own-initialized parameters. Original backbone tensor transfer, causal image/text and padding behavior, independent per-visit caches, complete/multitoken decode and checkpointed gradients are verified. Eight original training windows calibrate the new norm scales; calibration does not use validation data or preserve original outputs exactly.

The new actual comparison completed from our own warmup on the same verified systems corpus: **1,024 recurrent updates** versus **1,024/2,560 single-pass updates** on the same modified architecture. Its initial one/four-pass held NLL was **1.865457/2.606951**. Single-pass controls finish at **1.624056/1.525301**. The trained recurrent branch scores **1.889613 with one pass, 1.735413 with two and 1.738857 with four**. Additional passes now help this branch, but it remains worse than both single-pass controls. All trained branches still pass **0/24 functional and efficiency cases**; their syntax scores are 0/24, 1/24 and 0/24. The exact budgets and three completed checkpoint hashes are verified in `reports/conditioned-recurrent-code-ablation-summary-v1.json`. No policy is promoted, and the live main curriculum remains unchanged.

The [standalone measured-loss plot](reports/conditioned-recurrent-loss-comparison-v1.png) and matching PDF compare the trained recurrent model at each tested depth with both controls. They state the single seed, held sample size and compute-proxy limitation. An actual response audit retains all matching cases: 17 initial responses repeat a nonempty line at least four times, compared with 18 after the 1,024-update control and 16 after the longer control. Several shorter-control Python outputs use C++/Rust-style syntax; seven longer-control responses contain runs of at least 24 consecutive double quotes. These literal observations do not constitute a general language classifier (`reports/conditioned-language-response-drift-v1.json`). Native C++ assessment is running on the exact initial and trained policies; all 12 references pass, while the initial/shorter-control policies currently score 0/12 and 2/12 syntax with zero functional passes. Final native comparison remains pending.

The full native C++ comparison subsequently completed on the exact initial state and all three trained states. All four policies pass **0/12 functional and efficiency cases** under the fixed 512-token native-instruction budget. Syntax counts are **0/12 initially, 2/12 for ordinary 1,024, 0/12 for ordinary 2,560 and 0/12 for recurrent 1,024**. Both syntax-only cases extract to empty code and fail linkage because the requested functions are absent; they establish no usable syntax or runnable-solution gain. All 12 original reference functions pass their complete hidden and stress tests. Exact reconstructed policy-state identities, runtime/source hashes, every original task/signature and recomputed aggregates were verified (`reports/conditioned-recurrent-cpp-verification-v1.json`). Both experimental actors are terminal; main training and the Rust-guarded later joint/feedback controllers remain live. These results do not support a coding capability gain or replacing the production architecture.

Full native Rust retention is now installed before/after later joint learning and for both DPO and any reward-gated PPO branch. New joint/feedback Rust learning results are still queued.

The JIT profile installs filesystem/syscall restrictions before the target interpreter starts. Only a per-program temporary cache is writable. Persistent inability to inspect the cache stops the target; a bounded startup grace handles transient exec visibility. Eight focused checks pass (`reports/standalone-numba-v1-focused-tests-v4.log`), including real JIT/cache reuse, host/process/chmod denial, input/output/deadline bounds and cache-volume monitoring. Use the current self-contained model from `/home/jovyan/slm-lab`:

```bash
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES='' .venv/bin/python -m slm_lab.own_native_query_v1 \
  --checkpoint checkpoints/code-first-continuation-v3/standalone/model.pt \
  --prompt "Write a Python function to add two integers." --max-new-tokens 128
```

Add `--image /absolute/path/to/image.png --image-size 448` for an image question, `--raw-prompt` for verbatim completion, or `--json` for native token and runtime records. `--inspect` checks the artifact identity and declared metadata without constructing the full neural model. The named checkpoint is the completed code epoch; ongoing public pretraining writes separate resumable states. The interface does not establish improved model accuracy.
